- **ubuntu-operator**: Autonomous operator for multi-step admin work on one or more Ubuntu machines, local or over SSH. Use it for jobs that chain many commands with checks in between, for example "update all my servers and reboot the ones that need it", "deploy this compose stack", "move this service's data to the new disk", or "set up NFS mounts on these three hosts". It backs up before editing, validates before reloading, confirms anything destructive, and logs what it did. ([danielrosehill/Ubuntu-Admin](https://github.com/danielrosehill/Ubuntu-Admin/blob/HEAD/agents/ubuntu-operator.md))
- **ubuntu-system-agent**: Solves the #1 ROS 2 developer pain point: Ubuntu/ROS2 version coupling and setup issues. Catches missing source commands, wrong Ubuntu/ROS2 version pairing, systemd service issues, PATH problems, permission errors on /dev/*, missing udev rules for USB devices, wrong shell configurations (fish vs bash vs zsh). Use whenever Ubuntu system configuration affects ROS 2 functionality or setup fails. ([RiddheshMore/everything-ros2](https://github.com/RiddheshMore/everything-ros2/blob/HEAD/everything-ros2-claude-code/agents/ubuntu-system-agent.md))
- **ubuntu-troubleshooter**: Read-only investigator for a misbehaving Ubuntu machine, local or over SSH. It works through logs, services, resources, Docker, storage and network from evidence to a root cause, and returns a diagnosis with the supporting lines and a proposed fix without applying it. Use it when the cause is unknown and the search may cross several areas, for example "the server keeps going unresponsive", "containers randomly restart", "desktop freezes every afternoon", or "why did this box reboot". ([danielrosehill/Ubuntu-Admin](https://github.com/danielrosehill/Ubuntu-Admin/blob/HEAD/agents/ubuntu-troubleshooter.md))

- **udgt-citation-checker**: Fact-checks the citations in ONE UDGT teardown chapter -- every [[node-id]] against teardown.json, every path:line against the game checkout, and every quoted owner answer against answers.json -- and says which citations exist but do not support the sentence they sit under. Complements udgt.py check, which only proves that a [[id]] exists. Use this agent when the user asks to "design a UI page", "recommend components", "create a layout plan", "recommend design concepts/colors/fonts", or when the ui-design-guide skill needs deep design analysis. &lt;example&gt; Context: 사용자가 대시보드 페이지 설계를 요청 user: "대시보드 페이지 레이아웃 설계해줘" assistant: "UI consultant 에이전트를 호출하여 설계안을 생성하겠습니다." &lt;commentary&gt;UI 페이지 설계 요청이므로 ui-consultant를 호출한다.&lt;/commen… ([khalildhmine/M-Adrar](https://github.com/khalildhmine/M-Adrar/blob/HEAD/.claude/plugins/local/ui-designer/agents/ui-consultant.md))
- **ui-control-reviewer**: Reviews a diff for whether each interactive element is the right kind of control — clickable divs instead of buttons, links used for actions and buttons used for navigation, a switch where a checkbox belongs (or a switch that needs a Save button), radios vs select vs combobox at the wrong list length, a modal where inline or a page belongs, tabs vs accordion, two competing primary actions, a submit button disabled because the form is incomplete. Use when reviewing a diff that adds or changes interactive UI. ([galilei2050/belay](https://github.com/galilei2050/belay/blob/HEAD/plugins/usable-ui/agents/ui-control-reviewer.md) · ★2)
- **ui-controls-architect**: Use this agent when implementing manual control widgets and UI state management. Specializes in egui immediate mode GUI, interactive widgets, real-time updates, value validation, and emergency stop controls. Examples: &lt;example&gt;Context: Need manual control sliders user: 'Create responsive sliders with value validation' assistant: 'I'll implement egui sliders with real-time updates, clamping, and &lt;16ms response time using immediate mode patterns' &lt;commentary&gt;Expert in egui widgets, immediate mode UI, and real-time responsiveness&lt;/commentary&gt;&lt;/example&gt; &lt;example&gt;Context: Emergency stop button nee… ([wtyler2505/multi-controller-app](https://github.com/wtyler2505/multi-controller-app/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/ui-controls-architect.md) · ★2)
- **ui-controls-engineer**: Photo Jumper UI and controls specialist responsible for HUD, keyboard/touch input, intro/splash screens, debug overlays, on-screen buttons, and CSS styling. Use this agent for any work on user interface, player controls, or visual presentation. ([McFuzzySquirrel/photo-jumper](https://github.com/McFuzzySquirrel/photo-jumper/blob/HEAD/.github/agents/ui-controls-engineer.md) · ★2)
- **ui-conventions-reviewer**: features/・app/・components/ の UI 変更を、配置・stories・検証の選び方・見た目（DESIGN.md）の観点でレビューする。UI を追加・変更した後、PR 作成前に proactive に使用する。 ([engineer-first/idea-boost](https://github.com/engineer-first/idea-boost/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/ui-conventions-reviewer.md) · ★2)
- **ui-conventions-reviewer**: Read-only reviewer for Behera Bhada Khata UI conventions - layout-only pages, the MUI kit wrappers, theme colours, loading states, routes and flash params, formatted dates and labels, accessibility ids and the phone layout. Use when a change touches src/app, src/components or src/theme. ([priyaranjan-b/behera-bhada-khata](https://github.com/priyaranjan-b/behera-bhada-khata/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/ui-conventions-reviewer.md))
- **ui-conventions-scout**: Read-only conventions audit run ahead of the pipeline dashboard panel work. The subagent had no file-creation tool, so this report is written by the master agent on its behalf. ([alaamer12/MostaqlK](https://github.com/alaamer12/MostaqlK/blob/HEAD/.repertoire/agents/ui-conventions-scout.md))
- **ui-coordinator**: Use only for an explicit coordination-board task in the UI domain: briefs frontend_verifier and design_reviewer, merges their rendered-UI and design-review evidence, and escalates to the parent session. ([ucsahinn/agentchef](https://github.com/ucsahinn/agentchef/blob/HEAD/plugins/agentchef/agents/ui-coordinator.md) · ★7)
- **ui-copy**: Use this guide when you write or change text a person reads in the product: the web client, the desktop app and the mobile native app. It covers labels, buttons, placeholders, empty states, errors, notifications, permission prompts and the small dimmed hint, footer and caption lines under controls. English and Chinese follow the same rules. ([BotHarness/DeepSeekBot](https://github.com/BotHarness/DeepSeekBot/blob/HEAD/docs/agents/ui-copy.md) · ★932)
- **ui-copy**: UX copy specialist tuned to Uncapped's actual voice. Use this agent to write, review, or refine interface microcopy for any Uncapped prototype: buttons, errors, empty states, tooltips, onboarding, confirmations, notifications, forms, loading and success messages, modals, decline screens, repayment notices, AI/automated copy. The agent applies the voice signature extracted from the live Uncapped website (homepage.json, funding pages, how-it-works) and the in-product offer prototype, plus the lending-specific tone rules (trust, regulatory honesty, anti-anxiety on money-moving moments). Triggers… ([ana-ned/Sole-Prop-Flow](https://github.com/ana-ned/Sole-Prop-Flow/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/ui-copy.md))
- **ui-copy-reviewer**: Review user-facing copy and statically-detectable UX/accessibility in UI changes — flag developer jargon, AI-generated fluff, and clarity/a11y problems. Report findings only. ([VorobiovD/air](https://github.com/VorobiovD/air/blob/HEAD/plugins/air/agents/ui-copy-reviewer.md) · ★5)
